```
def coord_objective(t, base, alpha, beta, Mout, Srest, n):
    inner = base + t*alpha
    if beta nonzero: inner[center]+=t*t
    inner_max = float(inner.max())
    H = inner_max if inner_max>Mout else Mout
    Snew = Srest+t
    return 2*n*H/(Snew*Snew) if Snew>0 else inf
```

```
def adam_lse(u0, betas, time_limit, lr0=0.05, ...):
    n=len(u0)
    a=softmax(u0)
    M=score(a)/(2n) approximate; max_beta_to keep beta*M ~?
    best...
    for each beta in betas:
       m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
       start=time
       while time<start+alloc:
          f,g=compute_lse_grad_soft(u,beta) # a=softmax
          # normalize gradient? Maybe scale u by 1/n? Gradient components O? Could be large.
          grad = g (zero mean)
          t+=1
          m = (1-b1)*m + b1*grad
          v = (1-b2)*v + b2*grad*grad
          mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
          step = lr / (sqrt(vhat)+eps) * mhat
          # adaptive lr: target mean |u| < maybe 2.
          step_scale = lr * (0.05 / (np.linalg.norm(step)/np.sqrt(n)+eps))? hmm.
          u = u + step
          u -= u.mean()
          a=np.exp(u-u.max()); a/=a.sum()
          f_exact=score(a) maybe periodic
          update best
```

At uniform, q=1/n? L=0, w uniform? β small. Hard. We can compute norm and scale: Use gradient norm in log parameters: `gnorm = np.linalg.norm(g_u)/np.sqrt(n)`. Set step so typical |Δu| ~ step_target (e.g., 0.01) initially. `scale = step_target / (norm_g+1e-12)`. Then update u += scale*g_u. This is gradient descent with target relative step. Then adapt based on score change: if score worsens, reduce scale; if improves, increase (barzilai-borwein). This avoids fixed lr.

```

    max_load = max(load)
```

Let's define cross objective smoothed:
```
def cross_lse_grad_u(f, u, beta):
  g=softmax(u)
  conv_f_g = convolve(f,g) # length 2n-1, linear
  M=conv_f_g.max(); scaled=beta*(conv-M); L=M + logsum/beta
  weights w=exp/sum
  r = anti_conv(f,w)  # r_j = sum_i f_i w_{i+j}? Need formula: conv(f,g)_k = ∑_i f_i g_{k-i}. Derivative wrt g_j: ∑_i f_i for k=i+j => ∑_i f_i w_{i+j}. This is same t formula with a=f? r_j = ∑_k w_k f_{k-j} = anti_conv(f,w)
  # grad_f = r (degree1)
  g_dot_r = dot(g, r)
  grad_u = g*(r - g_dot_r)
  # Actually derivative of L wrt g. Since sum g=1, subtract g*dot.
  return L, grad_u
```
Note no normalization by S because sum1. f normalized.

LogSumExp stable:
```
def lse_from_values(vals, beta):
  v=vals.astype(float)
  m=v.max()
  e=np.exp(beta*(v-m))
  se=e.sum()
  L=m+np.log(se)/beta
  w=e/se
  return L,w
```
For beta large and n=7000, exp underflow okay. Need avoid beta*m overflow: beta maybe 1e5, m maybe 1e-4 =>10; fine. If unscaled, choose normalization. We'll normalize all sequences to sum=1.

Cross-subproblem objective L has units of g (sum1) conv load ~1/n. Beta scale: want beta * load differences O(10). load~1/n, so beta ~n. To approximate max, beta maybe 10*n? For n=7000, beta=70000-1e6. We'll anneal.

Self LSE objective uses q~1/n, beta also ~n*factor.

We can compute via weights anti? Simpler: choose coordinates from top contributions across peaks.
- Optimize active coordinates (d variables) with scipy minimize (L-BFGS-B bounds 0,∞) using exact score (or LSE with high beta) and others fixed. - After each subset optimizes, full a improved; repeat.

Block optimization loop:
```
def optimize_block_sets(a, time_deadline, rng, min_block=16, max_block=64, n_blocks=...):
 best=a.copy(); best_score=score(best)
 while time<deadline:
   # choose strategy round-robin
   if strategy gradient: idx=active_by_gradient(...)
   elif peaks: idx=active_by_peaks(...)
   elif random: idx=rng.choice(n,d,replace=False)
   elif window: idx=range(start,start+B)
   # maybe ensure idx size >1
   res=minimize(block_objective, best[idx], args=(best,idx,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds, options maxiter=200, ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-10)
   cand = best.copy(); cand[idx]=clip(res.x,0,inf)
   sc=score(cand)
   if sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc; log
   # else try smaller beta? 
```

```
def run_self_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, rng):
   u = np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); u-=mean
   best=a0.copy(); best_sc=score(a0)
   # betas based on M
   M=best_sc/(2n); beta_list = [5/M,15/M,50/M,200/M]
   for beta in beta_list:
       if time>limit break
       res=minimize(self_lse_u, u, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':200,'maxfun':800,'gtol':1e-8})
       cand=softmax(res.x); sc=score(cand); if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc; u=res.x-mean
   return best,best_sc
```

```
def run_cross_optimizer(a, time_limit, n_outer=3):
   f=a; best=a; best_sc=score(a)
   for outer in range(n_outer):
      beta = 50/M_f
      u0 = log(f)-mean
      res=minimize(cross_lse_u, u0, args=(f,beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options maxiter=200)
      g=softmax(res.x)
      # g minimizes cross. Update f with damping
      for d in [0.5,0.75,1.0]:
         cand=normalize((1-d)*f + d*g)
         sc=score(cand); if sc<best_sc: best=cand
      f=best.copy() # or damped from previous?
   return best
```
This consumes similar cost to self LBFGS but may provide global move. We can skip if time small. Use n_outer=1, maxfun=300.

Block search probably most important. Let's design robust block search top-level:
```
def block_search(a0, deadline, rng, max_blocks=None):
   n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0); best=a; best_sc=score(a)
   # choose beta based on M
   trial=0
   window_stride=0
   B = 24 if n<4000 else (32 if n<16000 else 16)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
      trial +=1
      if trial % 6 ==1: # sequential window
         # advance through sequence
         if max_blocks: window_stride = (window_stride % (n-B+1)); maybe random start but ordered
         start = (window_stride*B) % max(1,n-B+1)
         idx = np.arange(start, start+B)
         window_stride +=1
         strategy='window'
      elif trial %6 ==2:
         # reversed/random window
         start = int(rng.integers(0, n-B+1)); idx=...
      elif trial %6 ==3:
         idx=active_by_gradient(...)
      elif trial %6 ==4:
         idx=active_by_peaks(...)
      elif trial %6 ==5:
         idx=rng.choice(n, min(B,n), replace=False)
      else:
         # top two peaks windows union
      # limit idx size <= 64; if > random sample
      # Run block optimize
      cand = optimize_block(a, idx, beta, maxfun)
      sc=score(cand); if sc<best_sc: best=cand; best_sc=sc
   return best,best_sc
```
But `active_by_gradient` and `active_by_peaks` compute q each time; okay. We can cache q/M in loop? Easier compute in helper, some overhead.

```
def optimize_block(a, idx, beta, maxfun=100):
   n=len(a); d=len(idx)
   if d==0: return a
   upper = ...
   bounds = [(0.0, upper)]*d
   x0 = a[idx].copy()
   res = minimize(block_objective, x0, args=(a, idx, beta), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options={'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxls':20})
   cand = a.copy(); cand[idx]=np.clip(res.x,0,upper); return normalize(cand)
```

`self_lse_a`:
```
def self_lse_a(a,beta):
   n=len(a); S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return inf, zeros
   q=conv_full(a)
   qmax=q.max()
   scaled=beta*(q-qmax); e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum(); L=qmax+np.log(s)/beta; w=e/s
   t=anti_conv(a,w) length n
   r=2*t
   S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
   F=2.0*n*L/S2
   grad=2.0*n*(r/S2 - 2.0*L*a/S3)
   return F, grad
```

Interpolation helper:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
  N=len(a)
  if N==n: return a.copy()
  if N==1: c=a[0]; arr=c*ones(n); normalize
  x_old=np.arange(N); x_new=np.linspace(0,N-1,n)
  val=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
  val=np.maximum(val,0)
  if val.sum()==0: val=ones(n)
  return normalize_sequence(val)
```

```
def coord_exact_obj(t, base, alpha, beta, Mout, Srest, n):
   inner = base + t*alpha
   if np.any(beta): inner = inner + t*t*beta # beta one element
   im=inner.max()
   H = im if im>Mout else Mout
   Sn=Srest+t
   if Sn<1e-300: return 1e100
   return 2*n*H/(Sn*Sn)
```

`convolve_pairs`:
```
def convolve_pairs(x,y):
    ln=len(x)+len(y)-1
    if ln<=0: return np.array([])
    M=1 << (ln-1).bit_length()
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,M); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,M)
    return np.fft.irfft(X*Y,M)[:ln]
```
For large n, using rfft each call re-transforms a. For block objective, a_base changes active x each call; cannot pre-transform. OK.

`convolve_self(x): return convolve_pairs(x,x)`.

`anti_conv(a,w)`:
```
def anti_conv(a,w):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    return convolve_pairs(a[::-1], w)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length: conv length n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. Slice start n-1 length n ends 2n-2? `n-1:2*n-1` length n. Good.

`self_lse_a`:
```
def self_lse_a(a,beta):
   n=len(a)
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S<=1e-300:
      return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
   q=convolve_self(a)
   if q.size: q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   qmax=float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
   # For beta maybe adapt to qmax to avoid too high? 
   b=float(beta)
   if qmax <= 0: L=0; w=np.zeros_like(q)
   else:
      scaled=b*(q-qmax)
      # clamp scaled below -745 to avoid underflow? exp handles underflow but may warn? use np.exp(-np.minimum? ) no warning? Underflow warning maybe suppressed? We can `scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700, 0)`. But if all equal, scaled zeros. Clipping fine.
      scaled=np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
      e=np.exp(scaled); s=e.sum();
      if s<=0: # all underflow, set weights uniform over max
          maxmask=q==qmax; w=np.zeros_like(q); w[maxmask]=1/maxmask.sum()
          L=qmax
      else:
          w=e/s; L=qmax+float(np.log(s))/b
   t=anti_conv(a,w)
   r=2.0*t
   S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
   F=2.0*n*L/S2
   grad=2.0*n*(r/S2 - 2.0*L*a/S3)
   # Ensure finite
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad),grad,0)
   return F, grad
```
If beta large and qmax small, b*(q-qmax) maybe -70. If qmax 0, skip.

`cross_lse_u`: Compute g=softmax(u), `load=convolve_pairs(f,g)`, L,w, `r=anti_conv(f,w)`, `grad_a=r`, `G=dot(g,r)`, `grad_u=g*(r - G)`? Actually derivative of L wrt g = r. For u softmax, `grad_u = g*(r - dot(g,r))`. No 2n score factor; optimize L. Return `L, grad_u`. Subtract mean.

```
def sumexp_target(vals,beta):
```

Maybe define top-level `lse_values(vals,beta)` to reuse.
```
def lse_values(vals,beta):
   vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=float)
   if vals.size==0: return 0.0, np.array([])
   m=float(vals.max())
   scaled=beta*(vals-m)
   np.clip(scaled, -700, 0, out=scaled)
   e=np.exp(scaled)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0:
       mask=vals>=m-1e-15; w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[mask]=1.0/mask.sum(); return m,w
   w=e/s
   return m+float(np.log(s))/beta, w
```

`self_lse_u`:
```
def self_lse_u(u,beta):
   n=len(u)
   umax=u.max()
   a=np.exp(u-umax); S=a.sum(); a/=S
   F,grad_a=self_lse_a(a,beta)
   G=float(np.dot(a,grad_a))
   grad_u=a*grad_a - a*G
   grad_u -= grad_u.mean()
   # finite
   grad_u=np.where(np.isfinite(grad_u),grad_u,0.0)
   return F, grad_u
```

`cross_lse_u`:
```
def cross_lse_u(u,f,beta):
   n=len(u)
   umax=u.max(); g=np.exp(u-umax); g/=g.sum()
   load=convolve_pairs(f,g)
   load=np.maximum(load,0)
   L,w=lse_values(load,beta)
   r=anti_conv(f,w)
   G=float(np.dot(g,r))
   grad_u=g*(r-G)
   grad_u-=grad_u.mean()
   grad_u=np.where(np.isfinite(grad_u),grad_u,0.0)
   return L, grad_u
```

```
def run_block_search(a0, deadline, rng, slice_secs=60):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score_exact(a)
    n=len(a); B=24 if n<4000 else 32 if n<20000 else 16
    max_d = min(96, max(32, B*2))
    # beta tiers
    M=best_sc/(2*n); base_beta=min(1e7,max(100,50/M))
    tiers=[base_beta/5, base_beta, base_beta*5, base_beta*20]
    trial=0; win_start=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       trial +=1
       # Determine idx
       if trial % 7 ==1:
          start=win_start % max(1,n-B+1); idx=np.arange(start,start+B); win_start+=1
       elif trial %7 ==2:
          # window at random
          start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-B+1))); idx=np.arange(start,start+B)
       elif trial %7 ==3:
          beta=tiers[1 % len(tiers)]; idx=active_by_gradient(a,max_d,beta)
       elif trial %7 ==4:
          idx, M = active_by_peaks(a,max_d, min(20, 2*max_d))
       elif trial %7 ==5:
          idx=rng.choice(n,max_d,replace=False)
       elif trial %7 ==6:
          # two windows union
          start1=...; start2=...; idx=np.sort(np.unique(np.concatenate(...)))
       else:
          # top gradient+peaks union? maybe random gradient
          idx=active_by_gradient(a,max_d,tiers[0])
       if idx.size < max(2,min(B,n)):
          idx=rng.choice(n,min(B,n),replace=False)
       if idx.size > max_d:
          idx=idx[:max_d]
       beta=tiers[trial % len(tiers)]
       maxfun = 80 if n<10000 else 60
       cand=optimize_block(a, idx, beta, maxfun)
       sc=compute_score_exact(cand)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc; no_improve=0; print
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          # maybe still accept if LSE improved? not.
       if no_improve > 20:
          # randomize a bit: perturb best? Or change hi beta
          a = normalize(sequence with small Gaussian noise?) But block_search should operate on best? Use best for next? If candidate not improve, we can continue from best, not candidate. But sometimes small non-improvement in exact due to LSE, maybe candidate could lead to further improvement if kept. Could accept candidate with small worsening (annealing): if sc < best_sc*(1+1e-4) and trial%? accept. Simpler: if no_improve large, use perturbed best as a for next.
          p=best*(1+0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)); p=np.clip(p,0,None); a=normalize_sequence(p)
          no_improve=0
       else:
          a=best.copy() # refine from best
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
    return best,best_sc
```

```
def active_by_peaks(a,d,peak_count=20):
   q=convolve_self(a); q=np.maximum(q,0); M=float(q.max())
   if d>=len(a): return np.arange(len(a)), M
   pe=peak_count if q.size>peak_count else q.size
   peaks=np.argsort(q)[::-1][:pe]
   contrib=np.zeros(len(a))
   for k in peaks:

      lo=max(0,int(k)-len(a)+1); hi=min(len(a)-1,int(k))

         js=k - i_arr
         contrib[lo:hi+1] += w * (2.0 * a[js])

   return idx, M
```

```
def exact_one_coordinate(a, i, verbose=False):
```

```
def exact_one_coordinate(a,i):
  n=len(a); M=2*n-1
  conv=convolve_self(a); S=float(a.sum())
  k_lo=i; k_hi=min(M-1,i+n-1)
  if k_hi>=k_lo:
    ... outside_max as before.
    res=minimize_scalar(... bounds=(0,t_upper) options xatol=1e-10*max(S/n,1e-6))
    t=float(res.x)
    if t not finite: return a, compute_score_exact(a)
    a[i]=t; S=Srest+t
    conv[k_lo:k_hi+1] = base + t*alpha + t*t*beta
    inside_max=float(np.max(conv[k_lo:k_hi+1]))
    maxb=inside_max if inside_max>outside_max else outside_max
    return a, 2.0*n*maxb/(S*S)
```

```
def block_objective(x, a_base, idx, beta):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x))
    afull=a_base.copy()
    xx=np.clip(x,0.0,1000.0)
    afull[idx]=xx
    F,grad=self_lse_a(afull,beta)
    gidx=grad[idx]
    gidx=np.where(np.isfinite(gidx),gidx,0.0)
    return F,gidx
```
If d=0 not call. For d=1, L-BFGS-B on 1 variable with bounds okay. But `minimize` with `bounds` list length1.

`optimize_block`:
```
def optimize_block(a, idx, beta, maxfun=80):
    n=len(a); idx=np.unique(idx).astype(int)
    idx=idx[(idx>=0)&(idx<n)]
    d=len(idx); if d==0: return normalize_sequence(a)
    d=min(d,128)
    x0=a[idx].copy()
    upper = min(0.5, max(20.0/n, 5.0*float(np.max(a)) + 1e-6, 1e-3))
    bounds=[(0.0,upper)]*d
    try:
       res=minimize(block_objective, x0, args=(a,idx,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True,
                    options={'maxfun': maxfun, 'gtol':1e-8, 'ftol':1e-12, 'maxls':20})
       xopt=np.clip(res.x,0,upper)
    except Exception:
       xopt=x0
    cand=a.copy(); cand[idx]=xopt
    return normalize_sequence(cand)
```
Maybe `ftol` too strict; use 1e-12.

`run_self_lbfgs` details:
```
def run_self_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, max_betas=4):
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+time_limit
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score_exact(a)
    n=len(a); M=max(best_sc/(2*n),1e-12)
    betas=[5/M, 20/M, 80/M, 300/M][:max_betas]
    u=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); u-=u.mean()
    for beta in betas:
       if time>limit: break
       beta=min(1e7,max(100,beta))
       try: res=minimize(self_lse_u, u, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':300,'maxfun':800,'gtol':1e-8,'ftol':1e-12})
       except: break
       u=res.x; u-=u.mean()
       umax=u.max(); tmp=np.exp(u-umax); cand=tmp/tmp.sum()
       sc=compute_score_exact(cand)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc; u=u.copy()
       if time>limit: break
    return best,best_sc
```
Maybe after each beta, if no improvement, still continue with higher beta? Could. Good.

`run_cross_lbfgs`:

```
def run_cross_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, max_outer=2):
  a=normalize(a0); best=a; best_sc=...
  limit=start+time_limit
  f=a.copy()
  for outer in range(max_outer):
    if time>limit: break
    M=max(best_sc/(2n),...); beta=min(1e7,max(100,50/M))
    u0=log(f)-mean
    try res=minimize(cross_lse_u,u0,args=(f,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options maxfun=500)
    except: break
    g=softmax(res.x)
    for d in (0.3,0.6,1.0):
       cand=normalize((1-d)*f+d*g); sc=score; update best
    f=best.copy()
  return best
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400):
    start=time.monotonic(); total=max(60,float(budget_s)); hard=start+total-25
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+12345)
    best_a=np.ones(1024)*0.5; best_score=inf
    parent = load
    candidates=[]
    parent_len = len(parent) if parent else 0
    if parent is not None:
       pn=normalize_sequence(parent); candidates.append(pn)
       for L in (8192,12288):
          if L != parent_len: candidates.append(interpolate_sequence(pn,L))
    # shape seeds:
    n_shape = parent_len if parent_len>2048 else 8192
    if n_shape > 12288: n_shape=12288
    shapes=generate_shape_arrays(n_shape)
    for sh in shapes[:30]: candidates.append(sh)
    # maybe shape at 12288 if n_shape not
    if n_shape != 12288:
       sh2=generate_shape_arrays(12288); candidates.extend(sh2[:10])
    # random smooth seed
    a=rand_positive_smooth(n_shape,rng); candidates.append(a)
    # Evaluate candidates up to 40s
    gen_deadline=min(start+60, hard-300)
    cand_eval=[]
    for a in candidates:
        if time > gen_deadline and len(cand_eval)>10: break
        try:
           an=normalize_sequence(a)
           sc=compute_score_exact(an)
        except: continue
        if np.isfinite(sc):
           cand_eval.append((sc,an))
    # Sort
    if cand_eval:
       cand_eval.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    # Build pool: top, distinct lengths max4
    pool=[]
    for sc,a in cand_eval:
       # add if length not present or first 3 maybe
       length_exists=False
       for _,pa in pool:
           if len(pa)==len(a): length_exists=True; break
       if len(pool)<2 or not length_exists or sc < pool[-1][0] - 0.0005: ???
       Simpler: keep first top 3 distinct length.
       if len(pool) < 4 and not length_exists:
           pool.append([sc,a])
    # ensure at least best
    if not pool: pool=[[best_score,best_a]]
    for sc,a in pool[:?]: best update improved_candidate
    # Phases...
```

Let's write a helper `add_shape(out, arr)`? No nested but top-level:
```
def add_shape_to_list(out, arr, max_count):
    if len(out) >= max_count: return out
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr, 1e-12, None)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s>0: out.append(normalize_sequence(arr))
    return out
```
Then `generate_shape_arrays` calls it. This is top-level helper. Good.

Random smooth:
```
def random_positive_smooth(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # random Fourier coefficients small
   f=np.ones(n)
   for k in range(1,6):
       amp=0.05+0.2*rng.random()
       freq=k * (1+int(rng.integers(0,5)))? no nested, inline
       f += amp*np.cos(2π*freq*x + 2π*rng.random())
   f=np.clip(f,1e-8,None)
   # apply skew: f *= (1+2*x**p?)
   p=0.5+3*rng.random(); f *= (x+0.01)**p
   return normalize_sequence(f)
```

```
    best=0
    for i in range(1,len(pool)):
       if pool[i][0] < pool[best][0]: best=i
    return best
```

`blend_candidates`:
```
def blend_candidates(a,b, nsamp=21):
    a=normalize_sequence(a); b=normalize_sequence(b)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score_exact(a)
    L1=len(a); L2=len(b)
    lengths=[]; if L1==L2 lengths=[L1] else lengths=[L1,L2]
    for L in lengths:
        aa=a if L1==L else interpolate_sequence(a,L)
        bb=b if L2==L else interpolate_sequence(b,L)
        for t in np.linspace(0.0,1.0,nsamp):
            c=(1.0-t)*aa + t*bb
            sc=compute_score_exact(normalize_sequence(c))
            if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=c/sum
    return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def add_shape(out, arr, max_count): ... # top-level

def generate_shape_arrays(n, max_count=40):
    out=[]
    if n < 2: n=2
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    out_append = out.append? No lambdas. Use helper.
    add_shape(out,np.ones(n),max_count)
    for c in (2.0,5.0,20.0,100.0):
        add_shape(out,1.0+c*x,max_count)
        add_shape(out,1.0+c*(1.0-x),max_count)
    for p in (0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,20.0):
        add_shape(out,1e-8 + np.power(x+1e-12,p),max_count)
        add_shape(out,1e-8 + np.power(1.0-x+1e-12,p),max_count)
    for g in (1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0):
        add_shape(out,1e-8 + np.expm1(g*x),max_count)
        add_shape(out,1e-8 + np.expm1(g*(1.0-x)),max_count)
    for c in (0.01,0.05,0.2,1.0,4.0):
        add_shape(out,1.0/(x+c)+1.0, max_count)
        add_shape(out,1.0/(1.0-x+c)+1.0, max_count)
        add_shape(out,1.0/(x*(1.0-x)+c)+1.0, max_count)
    for f in (1,2,5):
        for ph in (0.0, 1.57079632679, 3.14159265358):
            add_shape(out,1.0+0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph),max_count)
    return out
```
Need ensure `np.expm1(30*x)` for x=1 -> 1e13 positive; okay.

```
def random_positive_smooth(n,rng):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  f=np.ones(n)
  for k in range(1,8):
    amp=0.05+0.25*rng.random()
    fr=k + int(rng.integers(0,4))
    ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    f += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*fr*x+ph)
  p=0.2+4.0*rng.random()
  f *= np.power(x+1e-6,p)
  f=np.clip(f,1e-8,None)
  return normalize_sequence(f)
```

```
def run_block_search(a0, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score_exact(a); cur=a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    n=len(a)
    B = 16 if n < 4000 else 24 if n < 12000 else 32
    if B > n: B=max(1,n)
    max_d = min(96, max(16, 2*B))
    M=best_sc/(2.0*n) if n>0 else 1e-6
    base_beta = min(1.0e7, max(100.0, 50.0/max(M,1e-12)))
    tiers = (base_beta/5.0, base_beta, base_beta*5.0, base_beta*20.0)
    trial=0; win_pos=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       trial += 1
       # if cur_score n/a
       strat = trial % 8
       if strat == 1:
          start = win_pos % max(1, n-B+1)
          idx=np.arange(start,start+B); win_pos +=1
       elif strat ==2:
          start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-B+1))); idx=np.arange(start,start+B)
       elif strat ==3:
          idx=active_by_gradient(cur, max_d, tiers[1])
       elif strat ==4:
          idx, M = active_by_peaks(cur, max_d, 20)
          if best_sc/(2*n) < M: pass
       elif strat ==5:
          idx=rng.choice(n, min(max_d,n), replace=False)
       elif strat ==6:
          s1=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-B+1))); s2=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-B+1)))
          idx=np.sort(np.unique(np.concatenate((np.arange(s1,s1+B), np.arange(s2,s2+B)))))
       elif strat ==7:
          # random small set
          idx=rng.choice(n, min(8,n), replace=False)
       else:
          idx=active_by_peaks(cur, max_d, 10)[0]
       if idx.size < 2:
          idx=rng.choice(n, min(B,n), replace=False)
       if idx.size > max_d:
          idx=idx[:max_d]
       beta=tiers[trial % len(tiers)]
       maxfun=60 if n > 10000 else 80
       cand=optimize_block(cur, idx, beta, maxfun)
       sc=compute_score_exact(cand)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best=cand; best_sc=sc; cur=best.copy(); cur_sc=sc; no_improve=0
          print(...)
       elif sc < cur_sc * (1.0 + 2e-5) and no_improve < 8:
          cur=cand; cur_sc=sc; no_improve +=1
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 12:
             noise=0.01 + 0.03*float(rng.random())
             cur=np.clip(best*(1.0+noise*rng.standard_normal(n)),0.0,None)
             sn=cur.sum()
             if sn>0: cur=cur/sn
             cur_sc=compute_score_exact(cur)
             no_improve=0
          else:
             cur=best.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0: break
    return best_sc,best
```

```python
"""
Hybrid non-negative low-peak-convolution searcher.

The objective is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
for a non-negative sequence a of length n.  The code works mainly with
sequences normalized to sum 1, then uses:

  1. multiple good initializations, including interpolation of the provided
     parent sequence and smooth skewed shapes;
  2. soft-max parameterized L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed objective;
  3. an alternating cross-convolution smoothing subproblem
     min_{g>0, sum(g)=1} logsumexp(conv(f,g));
  4. block-wise L-BFGS optimization over selected active coordinates;
  5. exact one-coordinate polishing on promising active coordinates.

The final sequence is normalized, clipped, and returned as a plain Python list.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
import operator
from operator import itemgetter
from scipy.optimize import minimize, minimize_scalar


def next_fft_length(length):
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (length - 1).bit_length()


def convolve_pairs(x, y):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_len = len(x) + len(y) - 1
    if out_len <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = next_fft_length(out_len)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, m)
    Y = np.fft.rfft(y, m)
    out = np.fft.irfft(X * Y, m)[:out_len]
    return out


def convolve_self(x):
    return convolve_pairs(x, x)


def anti_conv(a, w):
    """
    Returns r of length len(a) where
        r_i = sum_j a_j * w_{i+j},
    with w having length 2*len(a)-1.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = convolve_pairs(a[::-1], w)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    out = a / s
    return out


def compute_score_exact(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = convolve_self(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def improved_candidate(a, best_a, best_score):
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_score
    try:
        an = normalize_sequence(a)
        sc = compute_score_exact(an)
    except Exception:
        return best_a, best_score
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        print(f"  new candidate: score={sc:.10f} len={an.size}")
        return an, sc
    return best_a, best_score


def lse_values(vals, beta):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    m = float(vals.max())
    scaled = beta * (vals - m)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        mask = vals >= (m - 1e-13)
        if mask.sum() == 0:
            mask = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=bool)
            mask[int(np.argmax(vals))] = True
        w = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        w[mask] = 1.0 / float(mask.sum())
        return m, w
    w = e / s
    L = m + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    if not np.isfinite(L):
        L = m
    return float(L), w


def self_lse_a(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-18:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = convolve_self(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L, w = lse_values(c, beta)

    r = 2.0 * anti_conv(a, w)
    s2 = s * s
    s3 = s2 * s
    F = 2.0 * n * L / s2
    grad = 2.0 * n * (r / s2 - 2.0 * L * a / s3)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(F), grad


def softmax_of_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u = u - float(u.max())
    x = np.exp(u)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        x = np.ones_like(x)
        s = float(x.sum())
    return x / s


def self_lse_u(u, beta):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    u = u - float(u.mean())
    a = softmax_of_u(u)
    F, grad_a = self_lse_a(a, beta)
    if grad_a is None:
        return F, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_u = a * (grad_a - g)
    grad_u = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_u), grad_u, 0.0)
    grad_u = grad_u - float(grad_u.mean())
    return F, grad_u


def cross_lse_u(u, f, beta):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    u = u - float(u.mean())
    g = softmax_of_u(u)
    load = convolve_pairs(f, g)
    load = np.maximum(load, 0.0)
    L, w = lse_values(load, beta)
    r = anti_conv(f, w)
    rr = float(np.dot(g, r))
    grad_u = g * (r - rr)
    grad_u = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_u), grad_u, 0.0)
    grad_u = grad_u - float(grad_u.mean())
    return float(L), grad_u


def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]) / max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    old_x = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, float(a.size - 1), n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    if y.sum() <= 0:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_sequence(y)


def add_shape_to_list(out, arr, max_count):
    if len(out) >= max_count:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 1e-12, None)
    if arr.sum() <= 0:
        return
    out.append(normalize_sequence(arr))


def generate_shape_arrays(n, max_count=42):
    out = []
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return [np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)]
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    add_shape_to_list(out, np.ones(n), max_count)

    for c in (2.0, 5.0, 20.0, 100.0):
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + c * x, max_count)
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + c * (1.0 - x), max_count)

    for p in (0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 20.0):
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1e-8 + np.power(x + 1e-12, p), max_count)
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1e-8 + np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-12, p), max_count)

    for g in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0):
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1e-8 + np.expm1(g * x), max_count)
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1e-8 + np.expm1(g * (1.0 - x)), max_count)

    for c in (0.01, 0.05, 0.2, 1.0, 4.0):
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + 1.0 / (x + c), max_count)
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + 1.0 / (1.0 - x + c), max_count)
        add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + 1.0 / (x * (1.0 - x) + c), max_count)

    for f in (1, 2, 5):
        for ph in (0.0, 1.5707963267948966, 3.141592653589793):
            add_shape_to_list(out, 1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph), max_count)

    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    return out


def random_positive_smooth(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, 8):
        amp = 0.04 + 0.22 * float(rng.random())
        fr = k + int(rng.integers(0, 4))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        f += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * fr * x + ph)
    p = 0.2 + 4.0 * float(rng.random())
    f *= np.power(x + 1e-6, p)
    f = np.clip(f, 1e-8, None)
    return normalize_sequence(f)


def argmin_pool(pool):
    if len(pool) == 0:
        return -1
    best = 0
    for i in range(1, len(pool)):
        if pool[i][0] < pool[best][0]:
            best = i
    return best


def merge_candidate_into_pool(pool, sc, a, max_pool=4):
    an = normalize_sequence(a)
    try:
        sc = compute_score_exact(an)
    except Exception:
        return pool
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return pool

    for e in pool:
        if len(e[1]) == an.size:
            if sc < e[0]:
                e[0] = sc
                e[1] = an
            return pool

    if len(pool) < max_pool:
        pool.append([sc, an])
        return pool

    worst = 0
    for i in range(1, len(pool)):
        if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]:
            worst = i
    if sc < pool[worst][0] - 1e-12:
        pool[worst] = [sc, an]
    return pool


def blend_candidates(a, b, nsamp=21):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    b = normalize_sequence(b)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = np.inf

    L1 = a.size
    L2 = b.size
    lengths = [L1]
    if L2 != L1:
        lengths.append(L2)

    for L in lengths:
        aa = a if L == L1 else interpolate_sequence(a, L)
        bb = b if L == L2 else interpolate_sequence(b, L)
        for t in np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(nsamp)):
            c = (1.0 - t) * aa + t * bb
            cc = normalize_sequence(c)
            sc = compute_score_exact(cc)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cc
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def run_self_lbfgs(a0, time_limit):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a0.size
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, best_a

    M = max(best_sc / (2.0 * max(1, n)), 1e-12)
    betas = []
    for c in (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0):
        b = min(1.0e7, max(100.0, c / M))
        betas.append(b)

    u = np.log(np.clip(a0, 1e-300, None))
    u = u - float(u.mean())

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                self_lse_u,
                u,
                args=(beta,),
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                jac=True,
                options={
                    'maxiter': 250,
                    'maxfun': 450,
                    'gtol': 1e-8,
                    'ftol': 1e-13,
                    'maxls': 30,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
            break
        u = u - float(u.mean())
        cand = softmax_of_u(u)
        sc = compute_score_exact(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            print(f"    self-LSE beta={beta:.1f} score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_sc, best_a


def run_cross_lbfgs(a0, time_limit):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a0.size
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(best_a)
    f = a0.copy()
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, best_a

    for _outer in range(2):
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 3.0:
            break
        M = max(best_sc / (2.0 * max(1, n)), 1e-12)
        beta = min(1.0e7, max(100.0, 50.0 / M))
        u0 = np.log(np.clip(f, 1e-300, None))
        u0 = u0 - float(u0.mean())
        try:
            res = minimize(
                cross_lse_u,
                u0,
                args=(f, beta),
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                jac=True,
                options={
                    'maxiter': 200,
                    'maxfun': 350,
                    'gtol': 1e-8,
                    'ftol': 1e-13,
                    'maxls': 30,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        g = softmax_of_u(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
        for d in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            cand = normalize_sequence((1.0 - d) * f + d * g)
            sc = compute_score_exact(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                print(f"    cross-conv mix d={d:.2f} score={best_sc:.10f}")
        if best_sc < compute_score_exact(f):
            f = best_a.copy()
        else:
            f = normalize_sequence(0.7 * f + 0.3 * g)
    return best_sc, best_a


def active_by_gradient(a, d, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if d <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if d >= n:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    _, grad = self_lse_a(a, beta)
    if grad is None:
        return np.arange(min(d, n), dtype=int)
    idx = np.argsort(np.abs(grad))[::-1][:d]
    return idx.astype(int)


def active_by_peaks(a, d, peak_count=20):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if d <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int), 0.0
    if d >= n:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=int), 0.0
    c = convolve_self(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    peaks = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max(1, min(peak_count, c.size))]
    contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in peaks:
        k = int(k)
        w = float(c[k])
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            ia = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            js = k - ia
            contrib[lo:hi + 1] += w * (2.0 * a[js])
    idx = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:d]
    return idx.astype(int), M


def block_objective(x, a_base, idx, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return 1e100, None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    afull = a_base.copy()
    xx = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    afull[idx] = xx
    F, grad = self_lse_a(afull, beta)
    if grad is None:
        return F, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    gidx = grad[idx]
    gidx = np.where(np.isfinite(gidx), gidx, 0.0)
    return float(F), gidx


def optimize_block(a, idx, beta, maxfun=80):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int).ravel()
    n = a.size
    idx = idx[(idx >= 0) & (idx < n)]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(a)
    idx = np.unique(idx)
    idx = idx[:min(128, idx.size)]
    d = idx.size
    if d == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    x0 = a[idx].copy()
    avg = float(a.mean()) if n else 1e-3
    mx = float(a.max()) if n else 1e-3
    upper = min(
        1000.0,
        max(
            1e-3,
            20.0 / max(1.0, float(n)),
            5.0 * mx,
            10.0 * avg,
        )
    )
    bounds = [(0.0, upper)] * d
    try:
        res = minimize(
            block_objective,
            x0,
            args=(a, idx, beta),
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            bounds=bounds,
            jac=True,
            options={
                'maxfun': int(maxfun),
                'ftol': 1e-13,
                'gtol': 1e-9,
                'maxls': 25,
            }
        )
        xopt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        xopt = x0
    xopt = np.clip(xopt, 0.0, upper)
    cand = a.copy()
    cand[idx] = xopt
    return normalize_sequence(cand)


def run_block_search(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(best_a)
    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, best_a

    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf, best_a

    if n < 3000:
        B = 32
    elif n < 12000:
        B = 32
    else:
        B = 24
    B = max(2, min(B, n))
    max_d = min(112, max(24, 2 * B))

    M = max(best_sc / (2.0 * n), 1e-12)
    base_beta = min(1.0e7, max(100.0, 50.0 / M))
    tiers = (base_beta / 5.0, base_beta, base_beta * 5.0, base_beta * 20.0)

    trial = 0
    win_pos = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        trial += 1
        strat = trial % 8

        if strat == 1:
            start = win_pos % max(1, n - B + 1)
            idx = np.arange(start, start + B, dtype=int)
            win_pos += 1
        elif strat == 2:
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - B + 1)))
            idx = np.arange(start, start + B, dtype=int)
        elif strat == 3:
            idx = active_by_gradient(cur_a, max_d, tiers[1])
        elif strat == 4:
            idx, _M2 = active_by_peaks(cur_a, max_d, 20)
        elif strat == 5:
            idx = rng.choice(n, size=min(max_d, n), replace=False).astype(int)
        elif strat == 6:
            s1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - B + 1)))
            s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - B + 1)))
            idx = np.sort(np.unique(np.concatenate(
                (np.arange(s1, s1 + B), np.arange(s2, s2 + B))
            )).astype(int))
        elif strat == 7:
            idx = rng.choice(n, size=min(10, n), replace=False).astype(int)
        else:
            idx, _M3 = active_by_peaks(cur_a, max_d, 10)

        if idx.size < 2:
            idx = rng.choice(n, size=min(B, n), replace=False).astype(int)
        if idx.size > max_d:
            idx = idx[:max_d]

        beta = tiers[trial % len(tiers)]
        maxfun = 55 if n > 10000 else 75
        cand = optimize_block(cur_a, idx, beta, maxfun)
        sc = compute_score_exact(cand)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            cur_a = best_a.copy()
            cur_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"    block trial {trial}: {best_sc:.10f} len={n}")
        elif np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc * (1.0 + 3e-5) and no_improve < 10:
            cur_a = cand.copy()
            cur_sc = sc
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 14:
                noise = 0.008 + 0.03 * float(rng.random())
                cur_a = np.clip(best_a * (1.0 + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)), 0.0, None)
                ss = float(cur_a.sum())
                if ss > 0.0:
                    cur_a = cur_a / ss
                else:
                    cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_sc = compute_score_exact(cur_a)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break

    return float(best_sc), normalize_sequence(best_a)


def coord_exact_obj(t, base, alpha, center_idx, Mout, Srest, n):
    if t <= 0.0:
        t = 0.0
    val = base + t * alpha
    val[center_idx] += t * t
    im = float(np.max(val))
    H = im if im > Mout else Mout
    Snew = Srest + t
    if Snew <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100
    return float(2.0 * n * H / (Snew * Snew))


def exact_one_coordinate(a, i):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    Mconv = 2 * n - 1
    c = convolve_self(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    ai = float(a[i])
    k_lo = int(i)
    k_hi = int(min(Mconv - 1, i + n - 1))
    if k_hi < k_lo:
        return a, compute_score_exact(a)

    if k_lo > 0:
        m1 = float(np.max(c[:k_lo])) if k_lo > 0 else 0.0
    else:
        m1 = 0.0
    if k_hi < Mconv - 1:
        m2 = float(np.max(c[k_hi + 1:])) if k_hi + 1 < Mconv else 0.0
    else:
        m2 = 0.0
    Mout = max(m1, m2)

    L = k_hi - k_lo + 1
    ks = np.arange(k_lo, k_hi + 1, dtype=int)
    a_vals = a[ks - i]
    center_idx = int(2 * i - k_lo)

    base = c[k_lo:k_hi + 1].copy()
    old = 2.0 * ai * a_vals.copy()
    old[center_idx] = ai * ai
    base -= old

    alpha = 2.0 * a_vals.copy()
    alpha[center_idx] = 0.0

    Srest = S - ai
    maxb_now = float(np.max(c))
    avg = S / float(n)
    t_upper = min(
        1000.0,
        max(
            1e-6,
            10.0 * avg,
            10.0 * max(ai, 0.0),
            np.sqrt(10.0 * max(maxb_now, 0.0)) + 10.0 * avg,
        )
    )

    try:
        res = minimize_scalar(
            coord_exact_obj,
            args=(base, alpha, center_idx, Mout, Srest, n),
            bounds=(0.0, t_upper),
            method='bounded',
            options={'xatol': max(1e-12, 1e-9 * max(avg, 1e-6)), 'maxiter': 80}
        )
        tnew = float(res.x)
    except Exception:
        return a, compute_score_exact(a)

    if not np.isfinite(tnew):
        return a, compute_score_exact(a)

    out = a.copy()
    out[i] = tnew
    Snew = Srest + tnew
    c[k_lo:k_hi + 1] = base + tnew * alpha
    c[k_lo + center_idx] += tnew * tnew
    inside_max = float(np.max(c[k_lo:k_hi + 1]))
    mx = inside_max if inside_max > Mout else Mout
    sc = 2.0 * n * mx / (Snew * Snew)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return a, compute_score_exact(a)
    return out, float(sc)


def exact_coordinate_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(best_a)
    if n == 0 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, best_a

    max_count = int(min(220, max(50, n // 18)))
    rnd_count = int(min(70, max(20, n // 100)))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        idx_peaks, _ = active_by_peaks(a, max_count - rnd_count, 80)
        idx_rand = rng.choice(n, size=min(rnd_count, n), replace=False).astype(int)
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx_peaks.astype(int), idx_rand)))
        if idx.size > max_count:
            idx = idx[:max_count]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        idx = idx[rng.permutation(idx.size)]

        cur_a = a.copy()
        cur_sc = best_sc
        for i in idx:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            old_a = cur_a.copy()
            new_a, sc = exact_one_coordinate(cur_a, int(i))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= cur_sc + 1e-11:
                cur_a = new_a
                cur_sc = sc
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = cur_a.copy()
            else:
                cur_a = old_a

        cur_a = normalize_sequence(cur_a)
        cur_sc = compute_score_exact(cur_a)
        if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = cur_a.copy()
        a = best_a.copy()

    return float(best_sc), normalize_sequence(best_a)


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 3735928559)

    best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    best_sc = compute_score_exact(best_a)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if parent_obj is not None:
            p = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    candidates = []

    if parent is not None:
        pn = normalize_sequence(parent)
        candidates.append(pn)
        for L in (8192, 12288):
            if L != pn.size:
                candidates.append(interpolate_sequence(pn, L))
        if pn.size < 1024:
            for L in (2048, 4096):
                candidates.append(interpolate_sequence(pn, L))

    n_shape = parent.size if parent is not None else 8192
    if n_shape < 4096:
        n_shape = 8192
    if n_shape > 12288:
        n_shape = 12288

    shapes1 = generate_shape_arrays(n_shape, 34)
    candidates.extend(shapes1[:26])

    n_shape2 = 12288 if n_shape != 12288 else 8192
    shapes2 = generate_shape_arrays(n_shape2, 22)
    candidates.extend(shapes2[:10])

    candidates.append(random_positive_smooth(n_shape, rng))
    candidates.append(random_positive_smooth(n_shape2, rng))

    gen_deadline = min(hard_deadline, t_start + 90.0)
    evaluated = []
    for a in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline and len(evaluated) > 8:
            break
        try:
            an = normalize_sequence(a)
            sc = compute_score_exact(an)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            evaluated.append([float(sc), an])
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = an.copy()

    if evaluated:
        evaluated.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    pool = []
    for sc, an in evaluated:
        if len(pool) >= 4:
            break
        exists = False
        for _s, pa in pool:
            if pa.size == an.size:
                exists = True
                break
        if not exists:
            pool.append([sc, an])
    if len(pool) < 4:
        for sc, an in evaluated:
            if len(pool) >= 4:
                break
            pool.append([sc, an])
    if not pool:
        pool = [[best_sc, best_a]]

    for e in pool:
        if e[0] < best_sc:
            best_sc = e[0]
            best_a = e[1].copy()

    # Early phase: smoothed self-convolution optimization.
    phase1_end = min(hard_deadline, t_start + max(120.0, 0.12 * total_budget))
    print(f"Phase 1 self-LSE ends at +{phase1_end - t_start:.0f}s")
    for i in range(len(pool)):
        if time.monotonic() > phase1_end:
            break
        allowed = phase1_end - time.monotonic()
        if allowed < 15.0:
            break
        sc, aa = run_self_lbfgs(pool[i][1], min(150.0, allowed))
        pool[i] = [sc, aa]
        best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)

    # Alternating cross-convolution subproblems.
    phase2_end = min(hard_deadline, phase1_end + max(60.0, 0.06 * total_budget))
    print(f"Phase 2 cross-LSE ends at +{phase2_end - t_start:.0f}s")
    for i in range(len(pool)):
        if time.monotonic() > phase2_end:
            break
        allowed = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
        if allowed < 12.0:
            break
        sc, aa = run_cross_lbfgs(pool[i][1], min(90.0, allowed))
        pool[i] = [sc, aa]
        best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)

    # Main phase: block optimization and occasional candidate blending.
    phase3_end = min(hard_deadline, t_start + max(600.0, 0.82 * total_budget))
    print(f"Phase 3 block search ends at +{phase3_end - t_start:.0f}s")
    stall = 0
    last_best = best_sc
    while time.monotonic() < phase3_end:
        idx = argmin_pool(pool)
        if stall >= 2 and len(pool) > 1:
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(pool)))

        slice_end = min(phase3_end, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        if slice_end <= time.monotonic():
            slice_end = time.monotonic() + 15.0
        sc, aa = run_block_search(pool[idx][1], slice_end, rng)
        pool[idx] = [sc, aa]

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)
            stall = 0
        else:
            if best_sc < last_best - 1e-12:
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall % 2 == 0 and len(pool) > 1:
                    j = (idx + 1) % len(pool)
                    bsc, baa = blend_candidates(pool[idx][1], pool[j][1], nsamp=17)
                    pool = merge_candidate_into_pool(pool, bsc, baa, 4)
                    best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(baa, best_sc, best_a)
                    stall = 0
        last_best = best_sc
        if time.monotonic() > phase3_end - 5.0:
            break

    # Final phase: last chance block moves, then exact coordinate polish.
    if hard_deadline - phase3_end > 60.0:
        block_end = min(hard_deadline - 60.0, phase3_end + 0.5 * (hard_deadline - phase3_end))
        print(f"Final block end +{block_end - t_start:.0f}s")
        while time.monotonic() < block_end:
            slice_end = min(block_end, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
            if slice_end <= time.monotonic():
                break
            sc, aa = run_block_search(best_a, slice_end, rng)
            best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)

    exact_end = min(hard_deadline, t_start + total_budget - 8.0)
    print(f"Exact polish end +{exact_end - t_start:.0f}s")
    while time.monotonic() < exact_end - 1.0:
        slice_end = min(exact_end - 1.0, time.monotonic() + 110.0)
        if slice_end <= time.monotonic():
            break
        sc, aa = exact_coordinate_polish(best_a, slice_end, rng)
        best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 5.0:
        sc, aa = exact_coordinate_polish(best_a, hard_deadline - 2.0, rng)
        best_sc, best_a = improved_candidate(aa, best_sc, best_a)

    out_a = normalize_sequence(best_a)
    out_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in out_a]
    if sum(out_list) < 0.01:
        out_list = [0.5] * 128

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        out_list = [0.5] * 1024
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f} verified={verified:.10f} len={len(out_list)}")
    print(f"TIME used={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s / {total_budget:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out_list
```
